Chainalysis has reported a significant data breach where hackers obtained sensitive credentials and tokens from all its customers, including high-profile users such as government agencies and major corporations. The cyberattack utilized AI-assisted techniques and involved unauthorized access through an on-path data snatch, prompting the company to take down several services and mandate credential resets for affected users.

 - Hackers accessed sensitive user data including names, usernames, and password hashes.
 - The attack involved AI agents and an on-path data snatch, raising security concerns for high-profile clients.

Source: https://arstechnica.com/security/2026/11/chainalysis-says-hackers-stole-credentials-and-tokens-for-all-of-its-customers/

The project has its roots in a 14-year fundraising effort initiated by former basketball coach Park Seung-il, who was diagnosed with ALS and aimed to improve care options for patients.

The United States faced significant challenges and failures during its prolonged military engagement in Afghanistan, which ultimately led to its withdrawal and the Taliban's return to power. Key mistakes included an ineffective strategy focused on building a new Afghan government rather than leveraging existing local structures, a lack of adequate resources and attention diverted to Iraq, and insufficient pressure on Pakistan regarding its support for the Taliban.

 - The US aimed to create a centralized Afghan government rather than utilizing local governance structures.
 - US military and political attention was compromised by the war in Iraq, hindering efforts in Afghanistan.
 - The US struggled to manage its relationship with Pakistan, which undercut its objectives in Afghanistan.

Source: https://www.atlanticcouncil.org/?p=958640

Several key strategic missteps, including attempts to build a new political and military system without adequate resources and distractions caused by the Iraq War, contributed to the U.S. withdrawal and the Taliban's resurgence in power.

 - The U.S. aimed to create a new governance structure in Afghanistan but failed to account for historical and local power dynamics.
 - Distractions from the Iraq War and insufficient pressure on Pakistan allowed the Taliban to regroup and strengthen.

Source: https://www.atlanticcouncil.org/?p=958640

 - New state governors are implementing regulations that could pause data center projects, affecting Oracle's growth.
 - Oracle's current AI infrastructure and technology are under scrutiny as they age and become less competitive.
